# Skills · C · part 142

800 entries, cnn-architecture-accuracy-ranking-and-selection to co-scientist-ontology-enrichment. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **cnn-architecture-accuracy-ranking-and-selection**: Use when you must select among multiple CNN architectures by mean validation accuracy under StratifiedKFold cross-validation, as in CNNSplice's model selection over 11 candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cnn-architecture-accuracy-ranking-and-selection))
- **cnn-architecture-construction-for-rna-classification**: Use when a tissue-specific RNA m6A classifier must be built from 41-nt one-hot encoded sequences (164-dimensional vectors) in Keras/TensorFlow 2.0, e.g., reproducing im6A-TS-CNN model construction for one of the 11 species-tissue benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cnn-architecture-construction-for-rna-classification))
- **cnn-architecture-design**: Use when designing a convolutional neural network from scratch or via transfer learning for image classification, justifying every architectural choice. Covers conv/pool blocks, filters, kernel size, BatchNorm, dropout, the output layer, loss/optimizer, and transfer-learning backbones (MobileNetV2/ResNet50). Triggers when user mentions "diseñar CNN", "arquitectura convolucional", "Conv2D", "transfer learning", "MobileNet", "ResNet", "capa de salida". ([ignacio-aracena/Data-Science](https://github.com/ignacio-aracena/Data-Science/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cnn-architecture-design) · ★1)
- **cnn-architecture-design-for-chromatography**: Use when you have LC-HRMS profile-mode chromatograms with ground-truth peak annotations (reference features) and need to detect peaks in new samples with high sensitivity and specificity, especially when peaks exhibit variable morphology (left/right isomers), overlapping background signals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cnn-architecture-design-for-chromatography))
- **cnn-architecture-design-for-spectral-data**: Use when when you have mass-spectrometry data (m/z and intensity pairs or spectral matrices) paired with ground-truth molecular fingerprints or InChIKeys, and you need to learn a non-linear mapping from spectral patterns to structural fingerprints for downstream metabolite ranking or annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cnn-architecture-design-for-spectral-data) · 2 copies)
- **cnn-architecture-implementation**: Use when implementing or verifying the DESSO CNN in TensorFlow 1.1.0/Python 2.7 to predict TF–DNA binding from 690 ENCODE ChIP-seq datasets, or when validating that layer/parameter shapes match the published 16-filter, 32-neuron FC, single-sigmoid-output architecture. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cnn-architecture-implementation))
- **cnn-architecture-implementation-for-spectra**: Use when building or reproducing a CNN that classifies compound presence in 1D NMR mixture spectra despite chemical shift variation and peak overlap, and an augmentable spectral dataset with binary labels is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cnn-architecture-implementation-for-spectra))
- **cnn-architecture-implementation-tensorflow**: Use when a task asks to reproduce, extend, or validate the EpiCas-DL sgRNA-activity CNN — i.e. one-hot encoded 40-nt sequences plus min–max normalized epigenetic tracks must be mapped through a 150-filter multi-size convolutional layer in TensorFlow/Keras. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cnn-architecture-implementation-tensorflow))
- **cnn-architectures**: Plan and troubleshoot convolutional DARTS workflows for CIFAR-10 and ImageNet. ([VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill](https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/repositories/repo-skills/quark0-darts/sub-skills/cnn-architectures) · ★357 · 2 copies)
- **cnn-as-visual-system-model**: Skill for AI agent capabilities ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/vision-generative/cnn-visual-system-model) · ★4)
- **cnn-based-peak-quality-classification**: Use when QCing feature tables from LC-MS-based metabolomics runs where automated feature detection produces EICs that may include false positives, and manual peak-by-peak inspection is impractical. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cnn-based-peak-quality-classification))
- **cnn-bioactivity-classification**: Use when you have raw LC-MS/MS data for many biological samples (e.g., 545 wild soybean accessions) plus a binary or binned bioactivity endpoint (DPPH, elastase, TFC, TPC) and want end-to-end activity prediction without compound annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cnn-bioactivity-classification))
- **cnn-chromatographic-peak-classification**: Use when raw LC-HRMS profile-mode chromatograms (local maxima in rt x mz space) must be separated into true chromatographic peaks versus irrelevant background signals, and peak-center/bounding-box annotations are needed for downstream feature grouping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cnn-chromatographic-peak-classification))
- **cnn-edge-optimization-eval**: Evaluates the trade-offs between predictive accuracy, model compression, and dynamic inference efficiency of CNN optimization techniques (pruning, quantization, early-exit) for edge deployment. It probes how different architectures handle static compression versus input-adaptive latency reduction under hardware-constrained conditions. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Unspecified classification dataset, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy (%). ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/cnn-edge-optimization-eval) · ★3)
- **cnn-embedding-layer-design**: Use when building or re-instantiating a transformer basecaller that must project STFT spectrogram-like inputs of ONT dRNA current chunks into per-frame embeddings before attention encoder modules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cnn-embedding-layer-design))
- **cnn-error-estimator**: Supervised 2D CNN per-subcarrier error estimator under new/error_estimators/cnn/ (CNNErrorEstimator, train.py phases, TDL-A snapshots, current vs counterfactual label modes, antenna_snapshot vs time_freq domains). Use when editing cnn/train.py, smoke_validation.py, cluster_environment.yml, Phase 1–2 training/eval, unused-SC Huber / Phase 1b curves, the domain="time_freq" TDL-A/B/C x speed-bucket mode, or the pilot_policy/cnn.py + exp11_cnn_allocator.py consumers. For vec(H)/LMMSE/incremental loop use pilot-selection-simulator. Not the legacy old/ Model A (cnn_allocator). ([tal-pardo/model_based_pilot_allocation](https://github.com/tal-pardo/model_based_pilot_allocation/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cnn-error-estimator))
- **cnn-feature-extraction**: Use when spatial transcriptomics spots have paired H&E-stained slide imagery and morphological information must be converted into a per-spot feature matrix (e.g. morph_embeddings.csv) to enrich spatial domain identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cnn-feature-extraction))
- **cnn-hyperparameter-grid-search**: Use when training the pSCNN model on augmented NMR mixture spectra and you must determine which epochs, learning rate, and convolutional layer count yield the validation accuracy before final model selection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cnn-hyperparameter-grid-search))
- **cnn-image-classification-inference**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS samples have been encoded as single-channel grayscale molecular-fingerprint images and a trained CNN must predict a binary bioactivity label (e.g., DPPH high/low) with AUC/F1-style evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cnn-image-classification-inference))
- **cnn-image-classification-training**: Use when a collection of EIC plot images from LC-MS metabolomics runs must be labeled good/poor and a CNN classifier must be trained and deployed for peak-quality prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cnn-image-classification-training))
- **cnn-inference-for-peak-classification**: Use when detecting chromatographic peaks in LC-HRMS profile-mode data with a trained CNN model instead of heuristics, e.g. for peak-area quantification across replicate injections. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cnn-inference-for-peak-classification))
- **cnn-inference-on-metabolomics-data**: Use when you have LC-MS feature tables (with m/z and retention time columns) paired with raw .mzXML or .mzML files, and you need to automatically validate which detected features correspond to genuine chromatographic peaks versus false positives or noise. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cnn-inference-on-metabolomics-data) · 2 copies)
- **cnn-inference-on-spectral-data**: Use when you have preprocessed MS/MS spectral data (normalized peak intensities and m/z values) in memory or on disk, a trained CNN model checkpoint available, and you need to generate molecular embedding vectors for matching against a reference database of known metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cnn-inference-on-spectral-data) · 2 copies)
- **cnn-inference-on-variant-encodings**: Use when you have per-sample collapsed candidate VCFs from the ConsensuSV-ONT nextflow pipeline (vcfs output folder) and need to filter them down to high-quality SV calls via CNN scoring before benchmarking with Truvari. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cnn-inference-on-variant-encodings))
- **cnn-input-image-resizing**: Reference for selecting and applying the correct TensorFlow image-resize target dimensions when converting LC-MS/MS scatter fingerprints into fixed-size inputs for the MS2toImg CNN, especially when reproducing the wild soybean bioactivity benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cnn-input-image-resizing))
- **cnn-kernel-architecture-tuning**: Use when tuning a Keras/TensorFlow 1D-CNN that classifies promoter sequences: you need to sweep learning rate, kernel count, and kernel size and select the -performing configuration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cnn-kernel-architecture-tuning))
- **cnn-model-building**: Build CPU-safe PyTorch CNN layers and model helpers with MMCV builders, module bundles, wrappers, plugins, transformers, and complexity utilities. ([VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill](https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/repositories/repo-skills/mmcv/sub-skills/cnn-model-building) · ★357 · 2 copies)
- **cnn-model-evaluation-on-long-reads**: Use when comparing read-level 5mCpG detection performance of trained deep-learning models on long PacBio HiFi/CCS reads (≥10 Kb) against bisulfite ground truth and a baseline CNN caller such as primrose. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cnn-model-evaluation-on-long-reads))
- **cnn-model-inference-evaluation**: Use when testing a pretrained CNNSplice model (Model 1 .h5 weights from one organism) on a different organism's constructed splice-site dataset to measure cross-organism generalization accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cnn-model-inference-evaluation))
- **cnn-model-interpretation-nmr**: Use when a trained NMR spectrum-to-structure CNN (e.g., DeepSAT) needs interpretability: you want to know which regions of an HSQC spectrum drive fingerprint or class predictions, to validate that the model associates chemical-shift peaks with real functional groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cnn-model-interpretation-nmr))
- **cnn-model-performance-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking a deep learning classifier on RNA modification-site prediction data across balanced and imbalanced regimes and you need per-fold averaged metrics comparable to published baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cnn-model-performance-evaluation))
- **cnn-model-training-for-rna-modification-prediction**: Use when binary-classifying RNA modification sites from uridine-centered sequence fragments plus predicted secondary structure, or when reproducing/ablation-testing sequence-only vs structure-only CNN baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cnn-model-training-for-rna-modification-prediction))
- **cnn-model-training-for-tf-dna-binding**: Use when you have GC-matched ChIP-seq peak/background data (e.g. the 690 ENCODE datasets, 101-bp or 1001-bp windows) and need to train a CNN classifier of TF-DNA binding on sequence or DNAShape features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cnn-model-training-for-tf-dna-binding))
- **cnn-model-training-on-genomic-features**: Use when training supervised deep learning classifiers on ENCODE ChIP-seq peak data — either DNA sequence or DNA shape feature matrices — to later extract motifs and TFBSs, e.g. reproducing DESSO's 690-dataset benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cnn-model-training-on-genomic-features))
- **cnn-model-training-with-bayesian-hyperparameter-optimization**: Use when predicting sgRNA on-target editing efficiency for CRISPR-mediated epigenome editing from one-hot encoded 40 bp sequences plus min-max normalized epigenetic channels (nucleosome positioning, ATAC-seq accessibility, DNA methylation, RNA expression), and hyperparameters must be optimized. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cnn-model-training-with-bayesian-hyperparameter-optimization))
- **cnn-multi-task-fingerprint-inference**: Use when the input is a 1H-13C HSQC spectrum (normal or multiplicity-edited) as a peak list with 1H/13C shift pairs, and the goal is to infer a fingerprint, molecular weight, and structure class for later similarity search against chemical databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cnn-multi-task-fingerprint-inference))
- **cnn-multitask-model-evaluation**: Use when a trained NMR-based CNN must be validated on a held-out HSQC test set by reproducing reported Top-1 accuracy, fingerprint cosine similarity, and molecular-weight R-squared metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cnn-multitask-model-evaluation))
- **cnn-music-tagging-eval**: This evaluation benchmarks CNN-based models on automatic music tagging, measuring their ability to predict multiple genre, instrument, and mood labels from audio spectrograms. It assesses both standard classification performance and robustness to audio transformations like time-stretching and pitch shifting. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MagnaTagATune, Million Song Dataset, MTG-Jamendo, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports ROC-AUC. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/cnn-music-tagging-eval) · ★3)
- **cnn-regression-model-training**: Use when predicting continuous sgRNA gene-repression/activation activity from encoded sequence-plus-epigenetic matrices (11x40: 4 one-hot channels + nucleosome, chromatin accessibility, methylation, expression), or reproducing Fig. 2b-2c benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cnn-regression-model-training))
- **cnn-rnn-architecture-ablation**: Use when you need to demonstrate that both the convolutional and recurrent layer sets of Chiron's CNN+RNN+CTC architecture are essential, by training full vs. ablated models on the same raw-signal tfrecord data and comparing read-level accuracy against a reference genome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cnn-rnn-architecture-ablation))
- **cnn-rnn-ctc-neural-basecalling**: Use when raw nanopore fast5 electrical signals must be translated directly into nucleotide sequence without event segmentation, or when an open-source, trainable alternative to ONT's proprietary Albacore/Metrichor basecallers is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cnn-rnn-ctc-neural-basecalling))
- **cnn-sequence-model-inference**: Use when you need single-base-resolution functional impact scores (SAD = P(alt) − P(ref)) for non-coding SNPs across chromatin accessibility, histone mark, and TF binding profiles in a tissue-specific manner. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cnn-sequence-model-inference))
- **cnn-sequence-model-training**: Use when a candidate list of polyA/IPA sites must be scored purely from nucleotide sequence context, or when a CNN classifier for polyA sites must be trained and benchmarked with AUROC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cnn-sequence-model-training))
- **cnn-sgrna-activity-prediction**: Use when predicting sgRNA editing/knock-down or knock-up efficiency from a 40-nt target sequence plus genomic coordinates and epigenetic tracks (methylation, ATAC accessibility, RNA expression, nucleosome/TSS distance), e.g., screening dataset scoring or sgRNA design ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cnn-sgrna-activity-prediction))
- **cnn-snn-eeg-imagined-speech**: Hybrid CNN-SNN architecture for EEG-based imagined speech decoding. First integration of spiking neural networks into imagined speech BCI, achieving 80.13% accuracy on BCI Competition III benchmark. Activation: imagined speech, EEG decoding, CNN-SNN hybrid, spike-based BCI, neuromorphic BCI ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/neuroscience/cnn-snn-eeg-imagined-speech) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **cnn-snn-imagined-speech-decoding**: EEG-based imagined speech decoding using hybrid CNN-SNN architecture. First integration of spiking neural networks for imagined speech BCI, achieving 80.13% accuracy on BCI Competition III benchmark. Covers CNN feature extraction, SNN temporal classification, and neuromorphic BCI pipeline design. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/other/cnn-snn-imagined-speech-decoding) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **cnn-spectral-feature-extraction**: Use when you have preprocessed 1D NMR spectra (¹H and/or ¹³C) and need to extract spectral features for molecular structure inference on molecules with up to 19 heavy atoms. The skill is necessary as the first stage before fragment assembly or connectivity prediction; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cnn-spectral-feature-extraction) · 2 copies)
- **cnn-structural-variant-classification**: Use when calling deletion SVs from long-read (third-generation) BAM/SAM alignments where alignments can be converted to CIGAR-derived images and encoded sequences, and a deep model must fuse both modalities to output per-region deletion predictions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cnn-structural-variant-classification))
- **cnn-super-enhancer-classification**: Use when the task is to predict SEs vs TEs from raw DNA sequences (FASTA) alone, without ChIP-seq data, for one cell line, pooled cell lines, a held-out cell line, or across species (hg19 vs mm9). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cnn-super-enhancer-classification))
- **cnn-training-early-stopping**: Use when training a CNN classifier on one-hot encoded nucleotide sequences (e.g., 41-nt m6A windows) and you need a stable training regime with 5-fold cross-validation and early stopping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cnn-training-early-stopping))
- **cnn-training-instance-augmentation**: Use when training a custom PeakBot CNN from LC-HRMS profile-mode chromatograms with ≥100 matched reference features, and you must synthesize augmented peak/background training instances with bounded decoy overlap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cnn-training-instance-augmentation))
- **cnn-training-with-adam-optimizer**: Use when a pipeline needs to refine predicted splice-site positions from long-read (Iso-seq) alignments, or when reproducing TAGET's CNN splice-site correction trained on Ensembl-annotated exon/intron boundaries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cnn-training-with-adam-optimizer))
- **cnn-transfer-learning-fine-tuning**: Use when ion images (2D spatial intensity maps) from imaging MS must be automatically classified as off-sample/on-sample, and a small curated labeled set exists that is too large to tag manually but suitable for fine-tuning a pretrained vision model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cnn-transfer-learning-fine-tuning))
- **cnn-transformer-hybrid-architecture-design**: Use when when you need to detect and classify peaks in LC-MS regions of interest (ROIs) and simultaneously localize their boundaries for area integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cnn-transformer-hybrid-architecture-design) · 3 copies)
- **cnn-translate**: 把静态 INT8 QDQ ONNX（opset 13..19、N=1、affine-int8-v1）编译为指令流/参数包/DDR 镜像/模型专用 RTL/Vivado IP 源码包，并给出 G0~G6 分级验收命令与证据落点。 ([tudou-guo/edge-multiparadigm-nn-fpga](https://github.com/tudou-guo/edge-multiparadigm-nn-fpga/tree/HEAD/skill/skills/cnn-translate) · ★1)
- **cnn-variant-effect-prediction**: Use when you need to score the reference vs. alternative allele of a non-coding GWAS variant against chromatin profiles (DNase, histone marks, TF binding) in a tissue-specific manner, or to identify the motif driving a predicted effect (e.g., NFE2 at rs6426833). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cnn-variant-effect-prediction))
- **cnn-variant-quality-scoring**: Use when Oxford Nanopore long-read SV candidate calls from the six ConsensuSV-ONT callers need a quality score, i.e. candidate variants must be filtered via image-encoded evidence into a CNN probability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cnn-variant-quality-scoring))
- **cnn_allocator**: CNN pilot scorer sub-project (Model A): train_cnn_pilot_allocator.py, inference_cnn_pilot_allocator.py, TDL-A closed-loop eval, checkpoints/data caches. Use when editing CNN training/inference, PilotScorerModelA, CNNPilotSampler, or environment.yml. For vec(H)/LMMSE/pilot conventions use model-based-gaussian skill. ([tal-pardo/model_based_pilot_allocation](https://github.com/tal-pardo/model_based_pilot_allocation/tree/HEAD/old/.cursor/skills/cnn_allocator))
- **cnndm-eval**: Evaluates abstractive summarization quality by scoring generated summaries against human-written references and expert rubric-based scores. Use when the user types /co-scientist, asks to run the co-scientist, to generate, rank or test research hypotheses (also in Portuguese, e.g. "rodar o co-scientist", "gerar hipóteses de pesquisa", "ranquear hipóteses", "testar hipóteses do co-scientist"), or to continue, test or inspect an… ([rodolfo-viana/co-scientist](https://github.com/rodolfo-viana/co-scientist/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
